Clause 1.4 requires the governing instrument to be selected in this order: a current Technical Service Bulletin naming the asset’s serial range, then the applicable regional addendum, then the global policy. The India rate is **INR 1,320/hour through 31 March 2026** and **INR 1,450/hour from 1 April 2026 through 31 March 2027**. These rates are only conditional guidance because the repair date and operation code for this claim were not available.\n\nAccordingly, the eventual calculation should be:\n\n- **Labour:** `min(hours claimed, flat-rate hours for operation) × India rate applicable on repair date`\n- **Parts:** `quantity × India list price of part actually fitted`\n- **Partner uplift:** `INR 0`\n- **Total:** labour + parts\n\n## Next action\n\n1. **Warranty Operations / claim-system administrator:** retrieve or restore access to the C-2026-04124 claim record and provide the fields listed above.\n2. **Fabrikam Service Partners:** if the claim record itself lacks the repair detail, supply the actual-fitted part number, repair date, operation code, labour hours and any inspection report relevant to the failure or an exclusion.\n3. **Adjudicator:** keep the claim in an evidence/system-access hold; do not approve, decline or settle it until those records are available.\n\n## Draft record\n\nI could not record the required draft evidence request or draft adjudication because no service-claim-system read/write connector was available in this run.       "response": "## Coverage position — **cannot yet be finally decided; hold the claim, do not decline it**\n\nAsset **CIE-2200-AC-00602** is a 2200-series air compressor and its serial number falls within two current component-specific bulletin ranges. However, the accessible records did not expose the service-claim-system entry for **C-2026-04128**, so I could not establish the failed component, installed region, commissioning date, repair date, running hours at repair, service partner, claimed labour/parts, or evidence. Those facts determine which instrument applies and whether the repair occurred inside its coverage limit.\n\n### The potentially governing instruments\n\n1. **If this is a valve-plate repair:** apply **TSB-P-0112**. It covers 2200-series serials **CIE-2200-AC-00400 through CIE-2200-AC-00750**, including **00602**, and extends valve-plate coverage to **30 months from commissioning or 7,000 running hours, whichever occurs first**. It is effective from **9 February 2026**, supersedes TSB-P-0107, takes precedence over the regional addendum and global policy, and requires funding code **TSB** with reference **TSB-P-0112**.\n\n2. **If this is a drive-train/drive-motor repair and the asset is installed in EMEA:** apply **TSB-P-0115**. It covers EMEA 2200-series serials **00300 through 01100**, including **00602**, and extends drive-train coverage to **30 months from commissioning**. It is effective from **2 April 2026** and requires funding code **TSB** with reference **TSB-P-0115**.\n\n3. **If neither component-specific bulletin applies:** use the installed-region addendum over the Global Warranty Policy. The baseline is **24 months from commissioning or 6,000 running hours, whichever occurs first**; India varies this to **18 months or 5,000 hours**. The policy also says that without a commissioning record coverage cannot be determined—the claim must be held and the commissioning certificate requested; installation or despatch date must not be substituted.\n\n### Important precedence point\n\nDo **not** use TSB-P-0107. It is expressly superseded and has no effect. The current TSB-P-0112 narrowed the relevant serial range from ending at 00900 to ending at 00750; **00602 remains inside the current range**. The Warranty Policy Updates channel independently confirms that TSB-P-0112 superseded TSB-P-0107 and that the new range is 00400–00750.\n\n### Coverage-period test still required\n\nFor a valve-plate claim, compare:\n\n- repair date against **commissioning date + 30 months**; and\n- latest valid running-hours reading at or before repair against **7,000 hours**.\n\nCoverage ends when the first limit is reached. I could not perform that test because the claim-system dates and hours were unavailable.\n\nFor an EMEA drive-train claim, compare the repair date against **commissioning date + 30 months**. Installed region and failed component remain unestablished.\n\n### Amount payable\n\n**Not yet calculable.** The claim-system record and accessible library evidence did not establish the operation code, hours claimed, part actually fitted, quantity, partner, or repair date. Those are needed to calculate:\n\n- labour: lesser of the flat-rate allowance and hours claimed, at the applicable regional rate;\n- parts: applicable territory list price for the part actually fitted; and\n- any partner-specific uplift or other agreement term.\n\nNo amount should be drafted until those fields are verified.\n\n### Required next action\n\n**Warranty Operations / the adjudicator should place C-2026-04128 on evidence hold and retrieve or request:**\n\n1. failed component and repair description;\n2. installed region;\n3. commissioning certificate/date;\n4. repair date;\n5. latest running-hours reading at or before repair;\n6. operation code and claimed labour hours;\n7. part actually fitted and quantity;\n8. service partner and applicable agreement; and\n9. any inspection report raised.\n\nThen:\n\n- if valve plate and inside both TSB-P-0112 limits, draft **covered — TSB**, reference **TSB-P-0112**;\n- if EMEA drive train and within 30 months, draft **covered — TSB**, reference **TSB-P-0115**;\n- otherwise adjudicate under the applicable regional addendum/global policy, or decline only after the governing limit or an exclusion is evidenced.\n\n### Claim-system recording\n\nI **could not record the evidence hold or draft adjudication** because no service-claim-system read/write tool was available in this run. No draft was created. The requested fields above must be obtained from that system before a defensible final coverage decision or payment calculation can be made.\n\n## Sources\n\n1. `tsb0112` [TSB-P-0112 — Extended coverage, 2200-series valve plate](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/02-Bulletins/TSB-P-0112 Extended coverage - 2200-series valve plate (supersedes TSB-.docx>)\n2. `tsb0115` [TSB-P-0115 — Extended coverage, 2200-series drive motor, EMEA only](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/02-Bulletins/TSB-P-0115 Extended coverage - 2200-series drive motor, EMEA only.docx>)\n3. `globalpolicy` [POL-WAR-4.2 — Contoso Industrial Global Warranty Policy](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/01-Policy/POL-WAR-4.2 Contoso Industrial Global Warranty Policy v4.2.docx>)\n4. `tsb0107` [TSB-P-0107 — Superseded valve-plate bulletin](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/02-Bulletins/TSB-P-0107 Extended coverage - 2200-series valve plate.docx>)\n5. `teams0112` [Warranty Policy Updates — TSB-P-0112 supersedes TSB-P-0107](<https://teams.microsoft.com/l/message/19%3A2b6fa5f0482b434da9bced8da3a0c1e7%40thread.tacv2/1791005190191?groupId=1d67f268-f548-46ec-b5d0-ce84dc68e032&tenantId=72f988bf-86f1-41af-91ab-2d7cd011db47&createdTime=1791005190191&parentMessageId=1791005190191>)",

      "response": "## Decision — **cannot yet be decided; do not approve or decline before the partner call**\n\nI could not establish the claim’s asset, repair, warranty position, or payable amount from the records available to me. In particular, I could not access a claim-system record for **C-2026-04133**. The controlled Claim Evidence folder contains 12 inspection reports, but none is for C-2026-04133, and an exact Teams search returned no discussion for this claim.\n\n### What is established\n\n- **POL-WAR-4.2** is the global baseline and applies to claims submitted through the service claim system. Its standard period is **24 months from commissioning or 6,000 running hours, whichever occurs first**.\n- That baseline may be displaced under clause 1.4 by: **(1)** a current technical service bulletin naming the asset’s serial range, then **(2)** the regional addendum for the installation region, then **(3)** the global policy. The same policy says a missing commissioning record means coverage cannot be determined and the claim must be held while the commissioning certificate is requested.\n- **TSB-G-0029** says adjudication must use the claim-system record; the controlling hours figure is the latest reading held at or before the repair date. It also requires the claim to identify the part actually fitted. A missing inspection report alone is **not** enough to hold a claim unless an exclusion depends on the report.\n\n### Facts still required from the claim system\n\n1. Asset serial number and product family.\n2. Installation country/region and currency.\n3. Commissioning date or confirmation that the commissioning record is absent.\n4. Repair date and the latest running-hours reading at or before that date.\n5. Failed component, failure description and operation code.\n6. Parts claimed, quantities, and the part number actually fitted.\n7. Labour hours claimed.\n8. Service partner and any agreement-specific terms.\n9. Service history, prior repairs and any potentially relevant exclusion evidence.\n10. Submitted claim amount and supporting documents.\n\nUntil those facts are available, I cannot defensibly identify the governing warranty instrument, calculate the applicable coverage end point, place the repair inside or outside that period, or calculate labour and parts reimbursement.\n\n### Amount payable\n\n| Element | Current position |\n|---|---:|\n| Labour | **Undetermined** — operation code, claimed hours, partner, region and applicable rate are unavailable. |\n| Parts | **Undetermined** — fitted part number, quantity, territory list price and partner terms are unavailable. |\n| Partner-specific adjustments | **Undetermined** — partner identity/agreement is unavailable. |\n| **Total** | **Undetermined** |\n\n### Next action and owner\n\n- **Warranty Operations / adjudicator:** open C-2026-04133 in the service claim system and retrieve the ten items above. If the commissioning record is missing, place the claim on hold and request the commissioning certificate from the installing partner under POL-WAR-4.2 clause 2.3. If commissioning exists, apply the instrument-precedence test and calculate reimbursement from the current labour/parts schedules and partner agreement.\n- **Service partner:** supply only the genuinely missing evidence. Do **not** request an inspection report merely because one is absent; request it when the claimed failure or a potential exclusion makes it material.\n\n### Claim-system record\n\nI **did not record a draft adjudication or evidence request** because no service-claim-system connector or writable claim record was available in this run. Teams searches also returned no matching escalation or partner conversation. Without the claim record, I cannot establish the repair date, component/failure, operation code, latest running-hours reading at or before repair, commissioning date, fitted part, claimed labour, installation territory, or submitting partner. Those facts are required both to determine coverage and to calculate the payable amount.\n\n### Warranty position that can be established\n\nSerial **CIE-4000-CH-01201** falls within the inclusive range **CIE-4000-CH-01200–CIE-4000-CH-01850** in current bulletin **TSB-C-0051**. If the claimed repair concerns the **hydraulic circuit or compressor**, that bulletin governs ahead of the regional addendum and global policy and provides coverage for **36 months from commissioning or 8,000 running hours, whichever occurs first**. Claims allowed under it must use funding code **TSB** and reference **TSB-C-0051**.\n\nThis does **not**, by itself, establish that C-2026-04134 is covered: the bulletin applies only to hydraulic-circuit and compressor repairs, and the missing commissioning date, repair date, and qualifying running-hours reading are still needed. The global policy also says that where the commissioning record is absent, coverage cannot be determined and the claim must be held while the installing partner supplies the commissioning certificate.\n\n### Payable figure\n\n**Current defensible payable: not determinable.** No amount should be approved until the missing claim and asset facts are retrieved.\n\nFor orientation only—not an adjudication—if the missing claim proves to be an India hydraulic-pump replacement using operation **HYD-PUMP-RR** and fitted part **P-44120-A**, with a repair on or after 1 April 2026:\n\n- Labour allowance: **5.5 h × INR 1,450 = INR 7,975**. The rate card allows 5.5 hours for HYD-PUMP-RR and applies the lesser of claimed and flat-rate hours.\n- Part P-44120-A: **INR 186,400** at the India list price.\n- If the partner is **Fabrikam Service Partners**, its agreement gives no handling uplift: illustrative total **INR 194,375**.\n- If the partner is **Northwind Field Services**, its agreement adds 5% of parts only (**INR 9,320**): illustrative total **INR 203,695**.\n\nThese are conditional scenarios, not the payable figure for C-2026-04134, because the claim’s actual repair, date, part, hours, territory, and partner could not be established.\n\n### Next action\n\n1. **Warranty Operations / claim-system owner:** restore or provide access to claim C-2026-04134 and the asset registry entry for CIE-4000-CH-01201, including commissioning date, installation region, latest hours reading at or before repair, service history, repair date, operation code, fitted part, quantities, claimed hours, partner, and attached evidence.\n2. **Adjudicator:** place the claim on hold rather than approve or decline it until those records are available.\n3. If the commissioning date is missing, request the commissioning certificate from the installing partner under policy clause 2.3.\n\nI could not record the required hold/evidence request or draft adjudication in the claim system because no service-claim-system write connector is available in this task environment.\n\n## Sources\n\n1. `tsb0051` [TSB-C-0051 — Extended coverage: 4000-series hydraulic circuit and compressor](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/02-Bulletins/TSB-C-0051 Extended coverage - 4000-series hydraulic circuit and compre.docx>)\n2. `policy` [POL-WAR-4.2 — Contoso Industrial Global Warranty Policy v4.2](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/01-Policy/POL-WAR-4.2 Contoso Industrial Global Warranty Policy v4.2.docx>)\n3. `labour` [Warranty Labour Rate Card — FY26](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/03-RateCards/Warranty-Labour-Rate-Card-FY26.xlsx>)\n4. `parts` [Parts Price List — FY26](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/03-RateCards/Parts-Price-List-FY26.xlsx>)\n5. `fabrikam` [SPA-2023-FAB-IN — Fabrikam Service Partners](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/04-PartnerAgreements/SPA-2023-FAB-IN Fabrikam Service Partners.docx>)\n6. `northwind` [SPA-2024-NWD-IN — Northwind Field Services](<https://microsoftapc.sharepoint.com/teams/ContosoFieldService/Warranty Operations/04-PartnerAgreements/SPA-2024-NWD-IN Northwind Field Services.docx>)",
